| [[id:986BCDE6-1456-4E3F-ABA4-B01AB3AFE399][Try distcc on host newton (Debian on WSL) to shorten how lon…]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |                                                                               | Try distcc on host newton (Debian on WSL) to shorten how long each build holds a host build slot. Builds queue on the two host slots, the abstract AF_UNIX names @ores-build-slot-a and @ores-build-slot-b, across about ten worktrees, and a wait can last an hour while the compile takes minutes. ccache already hits 87.7 percent of compiles, so distcc only helps on misses, which are the costly files after a regenerated header. Conditions: newton needs the same clang as this host (Debian clang 21.1.8); plain distcc ships large preprocessed sources, and pump mode needs the same vcpkg tree and paths on newton; linking, preprocessing and all tests stay local; compass ties a slot to -j6, so a higher -j for the remote part needs new slot accounting so this host is not overloaded. Check the network path from WSL. Measure first: compare slot hold time for one build with and without distcc. Separately, the old slot-b lock file named bright_faraday since 2026-10-10 with a dead pid (200392). The abstract-socket lock cannot go stale because the kernel frees the name when the holder exits, and `compass build --status` reports the live holder plus, for a free slot, the last holder this checkout recorded; check that before adding tooling.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

The wrapper now stores them as debug log lines. Parse them into node samples so the grid screen can chart the memory each node uses.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| [[id:D0B8F570-25B7-4BC4-905D-077FFFA5E009][Twelve permission codes the iam handlers check are still not…]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | =iam= =permissions= =codegen=                                                 | Twelve permission codes the iam handlers check are still not seeded, so only the bare wildcard reaches those operations. check_handler_permissions.py derives the codes from the handler files and compares them to iam_permissions_populate.sql; iam is deliberately outside its REGISTRY because of this gap, which means the gate does not protect iam at all. The twelve are iam::account_contact_informations:write and :delete, iam::account_statuses:write and :delete, iam::account_types:write and :delete, iam::sessions:delete, iam::tenants:write, iam::tenant_statuses:write and :delete, and iam::tenant_types:write and :delete. Each is the same shape as the iam::roles:write gap that the bundle write closed: the generated handler asks for the resource's :write or :delete while the seed defines a different action for the same resource (for example iam::tenants:update, which the generated tenant write does not check). The seed is the side to move, because the generator emits <component>::<plural>:write and <component>::<plural>:delete unconditionally for every entity, so a handler cannot be given a different code without a model or template change. Closing all twelve lets iam join the REGISTRY in projects/ores.codegen/scripts/check_handler_permissions.py, after which the gate fails any future iam handler whose code no seed defines. The same audit is worth running for the other components that are still outside the registry.                        |

| [[id:207B3EA7-333C-4103-99F4-C2602164AD2A][Two components register the same shell command name, and the…]]                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |                                                                               | Two components register the same shell command name, and the first registration wins. The DQ shell part registers a menu called report_definitions through dq_commands::register_commands (repl.cpp:105), and the reporting part registers its own report_definitions menu later (repl.cpp:116). The DQ one takes precedence, so the reporting entity's shell commands are unreachable: a request for report_definitions list arrives at the DQ service as dq.v1.report_definitions.list, and the recipes generated for reporting's signatures are rejected by the DQ menu with 'Expected 8 arguments, got 9'. Nothing detects a duplicate menu name. Evidence: projects/ores.shell/application/src/app/repl.cpp:105 and :116, projects/ores.shell/application/src/app/commands/dq/dq_commands.cpp:48, and the reporting service log, which shows no request while the DQ service log shows the query.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
